**Answer: B.** The head subject is singular `list`; `of selected candidates` is a modifying phrase.

**Question 27:** Choose the correct sentence.

A. Neither of the files are complete.  
B. Neither of the files is complete.  
C. Neither files is complete.  
D. Neither of file are complete.

**Answer: B.** In formal usage, `neither` is ordinarily singular in this construction.

**Question 28:** Choose the correct preposition: `Citizens should have access ___ the service.`

A. for  
B. to  
C. on  
D. with

**Answer: B.** The noun collocation is `access to`.

**Question 29:** Choose the correct word: `The decision was made ___ the available evidence.`

A. according to  
B. according with  
C. according at  
D. according for

**Answer: A.** `According to` introduces the source or basis.

**Question 30:** Choose the correct sentence.

A. The staff discussed the schedule.  
B. The staff discussed about the schedule.  
C. The staff discussed on the schedule.  
D. The staff discussed to the schedule.

**Answer: A.** `Discuss` takes the topic as a direct object.

**Question 31:** Choose the correct word: `Please ___ the documents before signing.`

A. review  
B. revisal  
C. reviewer  
D. reviewing (without an auxiliary)

**Answer: A.** The sentence needs a base-form verb after `please`.

**Question 32:** Choose the correct sentence.

A. This rule applies to all offices.  
B. This rule applies for all offices.  
C. This rule applies at all offices (meaning coverage).  
D. This rule applies with all offices.

**Answer: A.** `Apply to` identifies the group covered by a rule.

**Question 33:** Which sentence has a clear pronoun reference?

A. The clerk told the officer that he was late.  
B. The clerk told the officer, “I am late.”  
C. The clerk told the officer that it was late.  
D. The clerk told the officer they were late, with no context.

**Answer: B.** The direct statement makes the speaker clear. The other options can leave the pronoun's referent uncertain.

**Question 34:** Choose the parallel sentence.

A. The plan aims to reduce delay, improving access, and clear standards.  
B. The plan aims to reduce delays, improve access, and publish clear standards.  
C. The plan aims at delays, to improve access, standards publishing.  
D. The plan aims reducing delay, access, and standards.

**Answer: B.** The three items use parallel verb forms: `reduce`, `improve`, `publish`.

**Question 35:** Choose the correct preposition: `The file was placed ___ the table.`

A. on  
B. into  
C. among  
D. since

**Answer: A.** `On` describes a surface position.

**Question 36:** Choose the correct sentence.

A. The applicants arrived at the office.  
B. The applicants arrived to the office.  
C. The applicants arrived on the office.  
D. The applicants arrived for the office.

**Answer: A.** `Arrive at` is used for a specific place such as an office.

**Question 37:** Choose the correct word: `Please ___ the officer of the change.`

A. say  
B. tell  
C. speak (without `to`)  
D. talk (without `to`)

**Answer: B.** `Tell` takes a person as its object: `tell the officer`.

**Question 38:** Choose the correct word: `The ___ of the scheme is to improve access.`

A. principal  
B. principle  
C. principally  
D. principled

**Answer: B.** `Principle` means a rule or guiding idea; `principal` means chief or primary.

**Question 39:** Choose the correct word: `The ___ officer approved the draft.`

A. principle  
B. principal  
C. principally  
D. principled

**Answer: B.** `Principal` can mean chief or main.

**Question 40:** Choose the correct preposition: `The office has operated ___ 2022.`

A. for  
B. since  
C. during  
D. by

**Answer: B.** `Since` gives the starting point; `for` would give a duration.

**Question 41:** Choose the correct preposition: `The office has operated ___ four years.`

A. since  
B. for  
C. from (without an end point)  
D. by

**Answer: B.** `For` marks the duration.

**Question 42:** Choose the correct sentence.

A. We entered the hall quietly.  
B. We entered into the hall quietly (ordinary movement).  
C. We entered to the hall quietly.  
D. We entered at the hall quietly.

**Answer: A.** `Enter` is transitive in the sense of going into a place; no `into` is needed here.

**Question 43:** In `The directions are subject to approval`, `subject` is used as:

A. a noun only  
B. an adjective in a fixed phrase  
C. a preposition  
D. an adverb

**Answer: B.** `Subject to` means dependent on or conditional upon approval.

**Question 44:** Choose the correct sentence.

A. An advice was provided.  
B. Advice was provided.  
C. Advices were provided.  
D. An advices was provided.

**Answer: B.** `Advice` is generally uncountable; use `a piece of advice` for one item.

**Question 45:** What is the best method for a preposition question?

A. Memorize one translation and apply it everywhere  
B. Read the full sentence, identify the relation and check the collocation  
C. Choose the longest option  
D. Ignore meaning

**Answer: B.** Prepositions depend on both meaning and established word partnerships.

## भाग 6: क्विक रिवीजन: स्मरणीय बिंदु एवं संदर्भ

### 6.1 फटाफट रिवीजन: 60 परीक्षा-उपयोगी तथ्य

1. RPSC 2026 Paper IV General English carries 70 marks.
2. Grammar & Usage carries 20 marks in the syllabus.
3. Prepositions are explicitly listed in Grammar & Usage.
4. The same word may act as different parts of speech.
5. Sentence position helps identify a word's grammatical job.
6. `Record` can be a noun or a verb.
7. `Present` can be a noun, verb, or adjective.
8. `Address` can be a noun or a verb.
9. `Subject to` means conditional upon or dependent on.
10. `Responsible for` is a common adjective-preposition collocation.
11. `Comply with` is the standard verb-preposition pattern.
12. `Refer to` points to a document or source.
13. `Depend on` names a dependency.
14. `Apply for` means seek a post or benefit.
15. `Apply to` can mean be relevant to a person or group.
16. `Consist of` introduces the parts of a whole.
17. `Access to` is the standard noun phrase.
18. `According to` introduces a source or basis.
19. `Discuss` normally takes a direct object without `about`.
20. `Enter the room` does not need `into` for simple movement.
21. `Prefer X to Y` is the standard comparison.
22. `At` often marks a point or exact time.
23. `On` often marks a day, date, or surface.
24. `In` often marks an area or a longer time period.
25. `Since` identifies a starting point.
26. `For` identifies a duration.
27. A singular subject ordinarily takes a singular present-tense verb.
28. A plural subject ordinarily takes a plural present-tense verb.
29. The head noun controls agreement, not a nearby noun in a modifying phrase.
30. `Each` is ordinarily singular as a subject.
31. `Neither` is ordinarily singular in formal `neither of` constructions.
32. Collective noun agreement should stay internally consistent.
33. Pronouns need an identifiable antecedent.
34. Repeat a noun when it removes real ambiguity.
35. Parallel items should use compatible grammatical forms.
36. `A` and `an` depend on initial sound, not initial letter.
37. `An honest officer` begins with a vowel sound.
38. `A useful report` begins with a consonant /y/ sound.
39. `Information` is generally uncountable.
40. `Advice` is generally uncountable.
41. Say `a piece of information`, not `an information`.
42. Use `fewer` with countable plural nouns in standard edited usage.
43. Use `less` with uncountable amounts in standard edited usage.
44. `Principal` can mean chief; `principle` means a rule or value.
45. `Advice` is a noun; `advise` is a verb.
46. A finished past time such as `yesterday` normally takes simple past.
47. Active voice often makes the responsible actor clear.
48. Passive voice can foreground the action or object.
49. Avoid passive wording when it hides a needed actor.
50. Concise verbs often make formal English clearer.
51. RPSC lists phrasal verbs and idioms in application.
52. RPSC lists one-word substitutes in application.
53. Confused or misused words are an explicit syllabus area.
54. The syllabus gives topic coverage but not a guaranteed question count per subtopic.
55. This chapter's exercises are not claimed as past RPSC questions.
56. Grammar choices should fit the sentence meaning.
57. Read the complete sentence before choosing a preposition.
58. Reject an answer that breaks agreement or collocation.
59. British and American usage may differ in some collective nouns.
60. Final editing should check meaning, grammar, reference, and punctuation.

### 6.2 अन्य पुस्तकों से जुड़ाव (Cross-References)

- **Chapter 8:** idioms, phrasal verbs, one-word substitutes, and commonly confused vocabulary.
- **Chapter 9:** comprehension and English précis use close reading and concise expression.
- **Chapter 10:** translation choices must preserve meaning and correct English syntax.
- **Chapter 11:** formal letters and reports apply grammar to practical communication.

**Chapter source:** RPSC, *Scheme & Syllabus of Examination*, dated 09-01-2026, Paper IV.

### अभ्यास प्रश्न

1. Choose the correct preposition: `She is responsible ___ maintaining the register.`
   A. of
   B. for
   C. at
   D. with
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The standard collocation is `responsible for` a task

2. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. The officer discussed about the proposal.
   B. The officer discussed the proposal.
   C. The officer discussed on the proposal.
   D. The officer discussed to the proposal.
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Discuss` takes a direct object in this meaning; `about` is unnecessary

3. In `Please record the decision`, the word `record` is a:
   A. noun
   B. verb
   C. adjective
   D. preposition
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** It names the action of entering or preserving the decision

4. In `Keep a record of the meeting`, `record` is a:
   A. noun
   B. verb
   C. conjunction
   D. adverb
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** It names a document or account

5. Choose the correct form: `Each applicant ___ a receipt.`
   A. receive
   B. receives
   C. receiving
   D. have received
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Each applicant` is grammatically singular

6. Choose the correct preposition: `The result depends ___ the evidence.`
   A. on
   B. at
   C. by
   D. for
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** The standard phrase is `depends on`

7. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. She applied for the post.
   B. She applied on the post.
   C. She applied at the post.
   D. She applied with the post.
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** A person applies `for` a position

8. Complete the sentence: `The rule applies ___ every department.`
   A. for
   B. to
   C. on
   D. from
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** A rule applies `to` a person, group, or case

9. Choose the correct form: `The committee ___ issued its report.`
   A. have
   B. has
   C. are
   D. were
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The sentence treats the committee as one body; singular agreement is consistent

10. Choose the correct article: `He submitted ___ useful report.`
   A. a
   B. an
   C. the
   D. no article
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `Useful` begins with a consonant sound /y/, so `a` is used

11. Choose the correct article: `She is ___ honest officer.`
   A. a
   B. an
   C. the
   D. no article
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The initial `h` is silent in `honest`, so the word begins with a vowel sound

12. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. The information are incomplete.
   B. The information is incomplete.
   C. The informations are incomplete.
   D. An information is incomplete.
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Information` is normally uncountable and takes a singular verb

13. Choose the correct preposition: `Please refer ___ the attached order.`
   A. to
   B. at
   C. on
   D. for
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** The phrase is `refer to` a document

14. Choose the correct word: `The panel consists ___ five members.`
   A. from
   B. of
   C. in
   D. with
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The standard phrase is `consists of`

15. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. The office has less applications today.
   B. The office has fewer applications today.
   C. The office has fewest application today.
   D. The office has lesser applications today.
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Applications` is a countable plural noun, so `fewer` is standard

16. Choose the correct form: `The office has ___ time this week.`
   A. fewer
   B. less
   C. few
   D. lesser than
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Time` is uncountable in this sense, so `less` is appropriate

17. In `The present plan needs review`, `present` is a/an:
   A. adjective
   B. verb
   C. noun
   D. preposition
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** It describes the noun `plan` and means current

18. In `They present the findings today`, `present` is a:
   A. noun
   B. verb
   C. article
   D. conjunction
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** It describes the action of formally showing or giving the findings

19. Choose the correct preposition: `The office is located ___ Jaipur.`
   A. in
   B. at (required for a city)
   C. on
   D. into
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `In Jaipur` is the usual form for a city. `At` can mark a specific point or institution, not the ordinary city phrase here

20. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. The meeting is on Monday.
   B. The meeting is in Monday.
   C. The meeting is at Monday.
   D. The meeting is by Monday (meaning scheduled to occur that day).
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `On` is used with a named day. Option D usually sets a deadline by that day

21. Choose the correct form: `The officer ___ the notice yesterday.`
   A. issues
   B. issued
   C. has issue
   D. issuing
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Yesterday` identifies a completed past time

22. Choose the clearer sentence.
   A. The review of the form will be undertaken by the team.
   B. The team will review the form.
   C. The form will have been being reviewed by the team.
   D. A reviewing of the form is by the team.
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The active sentence names the actor and action directly

23. Choose the correct preposition: `All staff must comply ___ the safety instructions.`
   A. with
   B. to
   C. at
   D. for
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** The verb `comply` is followed by `with`

24. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. She prefers tea than coffee.
   B. She prefers tea to coffee.
   C. She prefers tea from coffee.
   D. She prefers tea with coffee (when contrasting preference).
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The standard comparison is `prefer X to Y`

25. Which sentence uses `address` as a verb?
   A. Write the address on the envelope.
   B. The address is incomplete.
   C. The officer will address the grievance.
   D. The address was changed.
   **उत्तर:** C
   **व्याख्या:** Here `address` means deal with or respond to a problem

26. Choose the correct form: `The list of selected candidates ___ ready.`
   A. are
   B. is
   C. were
   D. have
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The head subject is singular `list`; `of selected candidates` is a modifying phrase

27. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. Neither of the files are complete.
   B. Neither of the files is complete.
   C. Neither files is complete.
   D. Neither of file are complete.
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** In formal usage, `neither` is ordinarily singular in this construction

28. Choose the correct preposition: `Citizens should have access ___ the service.`
   A. for
   B. to
   C. on
   D. with
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The noun collocation is `access to`

29. Choose the correct word: `The decision was made ___ the available evidence.`
   A. according to
   B. according with
   C. according at
   D. according for
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `According to` introduces the source or basis

30. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. The staff discussed the schedule.
   B. The staff discussed about the schedule.
   C. The staff discussed on the schedule.
   D. The staff discussed to the schedule.
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `Discuss` takes the topic as a direct object

31. Choose the correct word: `Please ___ the documents before signing.`
   A. review
   B. revisal
   C. reviewer
   D. reviewing (without an auxiliary)
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** The sentence needs a base-form verb after `please`

32. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. This rule applies to all offices.
   B. This rule applies for all offices.
   C. This rule applies at all offices (meaning coverage).
   D. This rule applies with all offices.
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `Apply to` identifies the group covered by a rule

33. Which sentence has a clear pronoun reference?
   A. The clerk told the officer that he was late.
   B. The clerk told the officer, “I am late.”
   C. The clerk told the officer that it was late.
   D. The clerk told the officer they were late, with no context.
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The direct statement makes the speaker clear. The other options can leave the pronoun's referent uncertain

34. Choose the parallel sentence.
   A. The plan aims to reduce delay, improving access, and clear standards.
   B. The plan aims to reduce delays, improve access, and publish clear standards.
   C. The plan aims at delays, to improve access, standards publishing.
   D. The plan aims reducing delay, access, and standards.
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** The three items use parallel verb forms: `reduce`, `improve`, `publish`

35. Choose the correct preposition: `The file was placed ___ the table.`
   A. on
   B. into
   C. among
   D. since
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `On` describes a surface position

36. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. The applicants arrived at the office.
   B. The applicants arrived to the office.
   C. The applicants arrived on the office.
   D. The applicants arrived for the office.
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `Arrive at` is used for a specific place such as an office

37. Choose the correct word: `Please ___ the officer of the change.`
   A. say
   B. tell
   C. speak (without `to`)
   D. talk (without `to`)
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Tell` takes a person as its object: `tell the officer`

38. Choose the correct word: `The ___ of the scheme is to improve access.`
   A. principal
   B. principle
   C. principally
   D. principled
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Principle` means a rule or guiding idea; `principal` means chief or primary

39. Choose the correct word: `The ___ officer approved the draft.`
   A. principle
   B. principal
   C. principally
   D. principled
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Principal` can mean chief or main

40. Choose the correct preposition: `The office has operated ___ 2022.`
   A. for
   B. since
   C. during
   D. by
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Since` gives the starting point; `for` would give a duration

41. Choose the correct preposition: `The office has operated ___ four years.`
   A. since
   B. for
   C. from (without an end point)
   D. by
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `For` marks the duration

42. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. We entered the hall quietly.
   B. We entered into the hall quietly (ordinary movement).
   C. We entered to the hall quietly.
   D. We entered at the hall quietly.
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** `Enter` is transitive in the sense of going into a place; no `into` is needed here

43. In `The directions are subject to approval`, `subject` is used as:
   A. a noun only
   B. an adjective in a fixed phrase
   C. a preposition
   D. an adverb
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Subject to` means dependent on or conditional upon approval

44. Choose the correct sentence.
   A. An advice was provided.
   B. Advice was provided.
   C. Advices were provided.
   D. An advices was provided.
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** `Advice` is generally uncountable; use `a piece of advice` for one item

45. What is the best method for a preposition question?
   A. Memorize one translation and apply it everywhere
   B. Read the full sentence, identify the relation and check the collocation
   C. Choose the longest option
   D. Ignore meaning
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** Prepositions depend on both meaning and established word partnerships ## भाग 6: क्विक रिवीजन: स्मरणीय बिंदु एवं संदर्भ ### 6.1 फटाफट रिवीजन: 60 परीक्षा-उपयोगी तथ्य 1.
